```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.

public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

```

- DO NOT modify the original source code. Only JML specifications are allowed to be modified.
- Every method must have at least one postcondition ("ensures" clause). Add more if needed to strengthen the specification.
- Every method must have a frame condition ("assigns" clause). Specify the exact frame condition or use "assigns \nothing" if no memory location is modified.
- Every loop must have loop invariants ("maintaining" clauses).
- Do NOT use assumptions ("assume" clause).
- Do NOT specify exceptional behavior ("signals" clauses). Exclude all exception-causing states via preconditions ("requires" clause).
- If the method returns a newly allocated object, state the freshness with "ensures \fresh(\result);".
- Provide a STRONG and COMPLETE specification: cover all functional behavior, state effects, and return value. The specification must contain enough detail to allow to reconstruct the original code unambiguously.


assistant: ```
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    /*@ 
      @ // Case 1: A is null or empty -> method returns null and does not modify memory
      @ public normal_behavior
      @   requires A == null || A.length == 0;
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @ 
      @ also
      @ 
      @ // Case 2: first row is null or empty -> method returns null and does not modify memory
      @ public normal_behavior
      @   requires A != null && A.length > 0 && (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0);
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @ 
      @ also
      @ 
      @ // Case 3: some later row is null or has different length -> method returns null and does not modify memory
      @ public normal_behavior
      @   requires A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0
      @         && (\exists int k; 1 <= k && k < A.length; A[k] == null || A[k].length != A[0].length);
      @   assigns \nothing;
      @   ensures \result == null;
      @ 
      @ also
      @ 
      @ // Case 4: A is a non-empty rectangular matrix -> method returns a fresh m-by-n matrix with entries rotated 180 degrees
      @ public normal_behavior
      @   requires A != null && A.length > 0 && A[0] != null && A[0].length > 0
      @         && (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < A.length; A[k] != null && A[k].length == A[0].length);
      @   assigns \result, \result[*], \result[*][*];
      @   ensures \result != null;
      @   ensures \fresh(\result);
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length; \fresh(\result[i]));
      @   ensures \result.length == A.length;
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length; \result[i].length == A[0].length);
      @   ensures (\forall int i,j; 0 <= i && i < A.length && 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
      @                \result[i][j] == A[A.length-1-i][A[0].length-1-j]);
      @   ensures (\forall int i,j; 0 <= i && i < A.length && 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
      @                A[i][j] == \old(A[i][j])); // A is not modified
      @ 
      @ // Behaviors are disjoint and cover all cases
      @ behavior;
      @*/
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        /*@ 
          @   // check that all rows are non-null and of the same length n
          @   loop_invariant 1 <= i && i <= m;
          @   loop_invariant (\forall int k; 1 <= k && k < i; A[k] != null && A[k].length == n);
          @   loop_writes i;
          @   decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        /*@ 
          @   // outer loop invariant: rows 0..i-1 of ret have been filled correctly
          @   loop_invariant 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @   loop_invariant (\forall int p; 0 <= p && p < i; (\forall int q; 0 <= q && q < n;
          @                     ret[p][q] == A[m-1-p][n-1-q]));
          @   loop_writes i, ret[*];
          @   decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            /*@
              @   // inner loop invariant: columns 0..j-1 of row i of ret have been filled correctly,
              @   // and rows 0..i-1 remain correct
              @   loop_invariant 0 <= j && j <= n;
              @   loop_invariant (\forall int p; 0 <= p && p < i; (\forall int q; 0 <= q && q < n;
              @                      ret[p][q] == A[m-1-p][n-1-q]));
              @   loop_invariant (\forall int q; 0 <= q && q < j; ret[i][q] == A[m-1-i][n-1-q]);
              @   loop_writes j, ret[*];
              @   decreases n - j;
              @*/
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}
```

      @ behavior;

        int n = A[0].length;

          @   loop_invariant 1 <= i && i <= m;
          @   loop_invariant (\forall int k; 1 <= k && k < i; A[k] != null && A[k].length == n);
          @   loop_writes i;
          @   decreases m - i;

                return null;

          @   loop_invariant 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @   loop_invariant (\forall int p; 0 <= p && p < i; (\forall int q; 0 <= q && q < n;
          @                     ret[p][q] == A[m-1-p][n-1-q]));

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;

        return true;
    }
}

public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        /*@
          @   // check that all rows are non-null and of the same length n
          @   loop_invariant 1 <= i && i <= m;
          @   loop_invariant (\forall int k; 1 <= k && k < i; A[k] != null && A[k].length == n);
          @   loop_writes i;
          @   decreases m - i;

                return null;

          @   decreases m - i;

    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        /*@ 
          @   // check that all rows are non-null and of the same length n
          @   loop_invariant 1 <= i && i <= m;
          @   loop_invariant (\forall int k; 1 <= k && k < i; A[k] != null && A[k].length == n);
          @   loop_writes i;
          @   decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        /*@ 
          @   // outer loop invariant: rows 0..i-1 of ret have been filled correctly
          @   loop_invariant 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @   loop_invariant (\forall int p; 0 <= p && p < i; (\forall int q; 0 <= q && q < n;
          @                     ret[p][q] == A[m-1-p][n-1-q]));
          @   loop_writes i, ret[*];
          @   decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            /*@
              @   // inner loop invariant: columns 0..j-1 of row i of ret have been filled correctly,
              @   // and rows 0..i-1 remain correct
              @   loop_invariant 0 <= j && j <= n;
              @   loop_invariant (\forall int p; 0 <= p && p < i; (\forall int q; 0 <= q && q < n;
              @                      ret[p][q] == A[m-1-p][n-1-q]));
              @   loop_invariant (\forall int q; 0 <= q && q < j; ret[i][q] == A[m-1-i][n-1-q]);
              @   loop_writes j, ret[*];
              @   decreases n - j;
              @*/
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;

              @   loop_writes j, ret[*][*];

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;

            return -1;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] != elem;
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
            if (arr[i] == elem)
                return i;
        return -1;
    }
}

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```
public class RotateMatrix180 {
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        /*@

          @   loop_invariant 1 <= i && i <= m;

          @   loop_writes i;
          @   decreases m - i;

          @   decreases m - i;
          @*/

              @   // inner loop: columns 0..j-1 of row i have been filled correctly

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

      @         && (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < A.length; A[k] != null && A[k].length == A[0].length);
      @   assigns \result, \result[*], \result[*][*];
      @   ensures \result != null;
      @   ensures \fresh(\result);
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length; \fresh(\result[i]));
      @   ensures \result.length == A.length;
      @   ensures (\forall int i; 0 <= i && i < A.length; \result[i].length == A[0].length);
      @   ensures (\forall int i,j; 0 <= i && i < A.length && 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
      @                \result[i][j] == A[A.length-1-i][A[0].length-1-j]);
      @   ensures (\forall int i,j; 0 <= i && i < A.length && 0 <= j && j < A[0].length;
      @                A[i][j] == \old(A[i][j])); // A is not modified
      @ 
      @ // Behaviors are disjoint and cover all cases
      @ behavior;
      @*/
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        /*@ 
          @   // check that all rows are non-null and of the same length n
          @   loop_invariant 1 <= i && i <= m;
          @   loop_invariant (\forall int k; 1 <= k && k < i; A[k] != null && A[k].length == n);
          @   loop_writes i;
          @   decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        /*@ 
          @   // outer loop invariant: rows 0..i-1 of ret have been filled correctly
          @   loop_invariant 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @   loop_invariant (\forall int p; 0 <= p && p < i; (\forall int q; 0 <= q && q < n;
          @                     ret[p][q] == A[m-1-p][n-1-q]));
          @   loop_writes i, ret[*];
          @   decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            /*@
              @   // inner loop invariant: columns 0..j-1 of row i of ret have been filled correctly,
              @   // and rows 0..i-1 remain correct
              @   loop_invariant 0 <= j && j <= n;
              @   loop_invariant (\forall int p; 0 <= p && p < i; (\forall int q; 0 <= q && q < n;
              @                      ret[p][q] == A[m-1-p][n-1-q]));
              @   loop_invariant (\forall int q; 0 <= q && q < j; ret[i][q] == A[m-1-i][n-1-q]);
              @   loop_writes j, ret[*];
              @   decreases n - j;
              @*/
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        return ret;
    }
}

public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;

    }
}

        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;

      @               A[i][j] == \old(A[i][j])); // A is not modified
      @*/
    public static int[][] rotateMatrix180(int[][] A) {
        if (A == null || A.length == 0) return null;
        if (A[0] == null || A[0].length == 0) return null;
        int m = A.length;
        int n = A[0].length;

        /*@
          @   // check that all rows are non-null and of the same length n
          @   loop_invariant 1 <= i && i <= m;
          @   loop_invariant (\forall int k; 1 <= k && k < i; A[k] != null && A[k].length == n);
          @   loop_writes i;
          @   decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 1; i < m; i++) {
            if (A[i] == null || A[i].length != n) {
                return null;
            }
        }

        int[][] ret = new int[m][n];

        /*@
          @   // outer loop: rows 0..i-1 of ret have been filled correctly
          @   loop_invariant 0 <= i && i <= m;
          @   loop_invariant (\forall int p; 0 <= p && p < i; (\forall int q; 0 <= q && q < n;
          @                     ret[p][q] == A[m - 1 - p][n - 1 - q]));
          @   loop_writes i, ret[*][*];
          @   decreases m - i;
          @*/
        for (int i = 0; i < m; i++) {
            /*@
              @   // inner loop: columns 0..j-1 of row i have been filled correctly
              @   loop_invariant 0 <= j && j <= n;
              @   loop_invariant (\forall int p; 0 <= p && p < i; (\forall int q; 0 <= q && q < n;
              @                      ret[p][q] == A[m - 1 - p][n - 1 - q]));
              @   loop_invariant (\forall int q; 0 <= q && q < j; ret[i][q] == A[m - 1 - i][n - 1 - q]);
              @   loop_writes j, ret[*][*];
              @   decreases n - j;
              @*/
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) {
                ret[i][j] = A[m-1-i][n-1-j];
            }
        }

        /* Help the prover: relate ret bounds to A using pre-state lengths */
        //@ assert ret.length == m;
        //@ assert (\forall int ii; 0 <= ii && ii < m; ret[ii].length == n);
        //@ assert (\forall int ii,jj; 0 <= ii && ii < m && 0 <= jj && jj < n;
        //@            ret[ii][jj] == A[m-1-ii][n-1-jj]);

        return ret;
    }
